HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001219/B008"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_10575c6fd067ff3ce8fd","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:1:1\",\"root_001219\",[\"B008\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Sonraki dağılıp çözülmeler, bu darbenin yerleşik biçimleri çıplak bırakan bir söküm gücü taşıdığını gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001219/B001","root_001219/B004","root_001219/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_26d6e408d136b9645846","text":"The repetition and questions in 101:2-3 confirmed that `ق ر ع B006` operates at discourse level before any physical scene appears. The transformations in 101:4-5 then supplied the delayed evidence for `ق ر ع B001` and `ق ر ع B008`. Finally, the first `فَأَمَّا مَن` scale clause in 101:6 weakly activated `ق ر ع B004`, decisive apportionment: weighing is not lot-casting, but the event now also begins to allocate a case.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001219/B001","root_001219/B004","root_001219/B006","root_001219/B008","root_001219/B010"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_27e16ca7f318f41d223a","text":"1. The blow that opens the whole mechanism. Evidence: ق ر ع B001, striking one thing against another; ق ر ع B006, warning/rebuke that checks the hearer. Structural cue: the one-word nominal opening is repeated in 101:2 and made unknowable in 101:3. Mechanism: impact first arrives as a name, then as an interrogated object, then as an epistemic shock. Abductive move: I supply the causal movement from sound-impact to cognitive arrest. Reading change: القارعة is not only an event-label; it is a blow whose first target is attention and recognition. 2. A public exposed place. Evidence: ق ر ع B010, open high surface of road/courtyard; ق ر ع B008/B011, bare or scraped surface. Structural cue: 101:4-5 display people and mountains stripped from stable places into visible dispersed matter. Mechanism: the \"striking\" event also creates an exposed arena where coverings and settled forms are removed. Abductive move: I infer that repeated naming turns the event into the place/plane on which later transformations appear. Reading change: القارعة can be read spatially as the exposed surface of manifestation, not only temporally as a coming disaster. 3. A selector before the scales. Evidence: ق ر ع B004, casting lots/drawing shares; و ز ن B002 in 101:6/8, balance of justice; ث ق ل B001 and خ ف ف B001, heaviness/lightness. Structural cue: the surah moves from the repeated قارعة to paired outcomes by weight. Mechanism: the striking event functions like a decisive selection process that separates shares into heavy and light cases. Abductive move: I supply the legal-apportioning analogy between lot-casting and weighing. Reading change: القارعة becomes the onset of adjudicative sorting.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001219/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_43f18f47b212e2ee0fc4","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:1:1\",\"root_001219\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Sonraki ağır-hafif ayrımı, ilk darbeye payları ayıran bir seçme işlevi de surprised me by activating ق ر ع B004: the first \"striking\" also behaves like a selector before the scales. 101:4-5 also made ق ر ع B010/B008 visible: the blow creates an exposed, stripped field where people and mountains are displayed.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001219/B008","root_001219/B010","root_001219/B011"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_78cf3012751dc07326c6","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:1:1\",\"root_001219\",[\"B008\",\"B010\",\"B011\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Olayın adı, açıklamadan önce dinleyeni durdurup dikkat kesilmeye zorlayan bir uyarı edimi de olur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001219/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_891ea73dfba25140d7aa","text":"3. **Exploratory stripping of established structure.** `ق ر ع B008` carries bareness, stripping, and emptied enclosures. People are scattered in 101:4, while mountains are converted from compact masses into fluffed wool in 101:5. The branch acts as the event's de-structuring operation: social and geological aggregates lose the coverings or cohesion by which they appeared stable. The causal assignment to `ٱلْقَارِعَةُ` is inferred from sequence rather than stated. On this activation, the noun names not only a collision but an occurrence that leaves established forms bare.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001219/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_aa793790b4ffa4ebd686","text":"1. **Impact propagated through unlike bodies.** `ق ر ع B001` supplies striking one thing against another. The isolated noun is followed by its repetition, two questions, then parallel transformations of people and mountains in 101:4-5. The branch functions as an initiating impact whose effects propagate through both living movement and solid mass. The packet supplies no explicit impact arrow between the title and those transformations, so that transmission is the abductive link. The one-word ayah reads less as a title alone and more as the first blow whose consequences the following ayat display.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001219/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_bb8809ee2aa14a2de9aa","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:1:1\",\"root_001219\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Tek sözcüklü ad, etkileri insan kalabalığına ve dağların yapısına yayılan ilk darbe gibi işler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001219/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e7d7b29b9798cc59aa84","text":"2. **A warning that arrests before it explains.** `ق ر ع B006` is \"restraining by a striking warning.\" Isolation in 101:1, repetition in 101:2-3, and delayed description create a discourse sequence of interruption before disclosure. This branch gives the noun the role of checking the hearer and forcing attention. I infer that formal delay performs the restraint described by the branch; the text supplies repetition and questioning but does not label their effect. `ٱلْقَارِعَةُ` therefore reads simultaneously as an event and as an utterance that strikes its audience into attention.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
